## Sheet: Survey_vNov20
|  | Facilitating the financial inclusion of Afro-descendants and indigenous peoples in Colombia |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
|  | Introduction:  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Survey objective: | This study seeks to analyze the living conditions, expectations, opportunities, and barriers that NARP and indigenous communities face in accessing financial services. This aims to identify specific needs and propose solutions that contribute to improving the economic well-being and financial inclusion of these communities. Participation consists of answering a survey that will take approximately 35 minutes. The questions will cover topics such as access to financial products and services, the current economic situation, sources of income, and the challenges and support required in terms of financial inclusion. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Target population:  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Confidentiality agreement  | The information collected will be treated strictly confidentially and will be stored on secure servers to which only the research team will have access. The results will be presented in aggregate form to avoid identification of participants. Equilibrium SDC respects the identity, culture and particularities of ethnic communities, and complies with Law 1581 of 2012 on the protection of personal data, as well as ILO Convention 169, guaranteeing the rights of communities. In addition, it undertakes to protect data against unauthorized access, although it clarifies that it cannot guarantee protection against computer attacks. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |

## Sheet: Phase 1+2 Baseline
|  | Introduction: | Good morning/Good afternoon Sir/Madam. My name is………………. I am a researcher working with consultants from Kampala. I would like to ask you afew questions about your role as the Local Council chairperson of (Name the village). Your name was randomly selected from all the villages from (Distric name). Meaning, every chairperson in this district had an equal chance of being selected to participate in this interview. All your responses will be treated with utmost confidentiality; meaning that your name or response will not be attributed to your identity. Also, there are no wrong or right answers, its all about your honest opinion. There is no direct benefit for participating in this study but rather the information you give us will be used to inform future programs aimed at strengthening LC roles. This interview will take approximately one hour. So, can I go ahead and interview you? Thank you. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
